template$<$typename Env$>$ \\
void vt::\+envelope\+Set\+Is\+Locked (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{Env \&}]{env}{, }\item[{bool}]{is\+\_\+locked}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}}



Set whether this message\textquotesingle{}s envelope is locked. 

A locked message will prevent key parts of the envelope from being updated with a guard assert. This is to prevent accidental edge-\/cases such as sending a message twice.

A message is locked when it is sent and received. Unlocking messages should be reserved for special cases as done in some core code.


\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\doxymbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em env} & the envelope \\
\hline
\doxymbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em is\+\_\+locked} & value indicating if message is locked \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

void vt::\+envelope\+Unlock\+For\+Forwarding (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{Env \&}]{env}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}}



Unlock an envelope, denoting the use is a proxy handler. 

A received message is normally locked to discourage the re-\/usage of messages supplied to handlers. However, some proxy/\+forwarding handlers re-\/use the original message.

This is equivalent to calling {\ttfamily envelope\+Set\+Is\+Locked} with false and should generally be set inside the message handler itself.

\doxymbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em env} & the envelope \\

Create a new message. 

Create a new message already wrapped in a \doxylink{namespacevt_a0f8ba6b65b1d630ab97265dcc90369a9}{Msg\+Ptr}. The arguments are forwarded down to the underlying message\textquotesingle{}s constructor.

The lifetime of the message is controlled by \doxylink{namespacevt_a0f8ba6b65b1d630ab97265dcc90369a9}{Msg\+Ptr} and will be destroyed when the returned \doxylink{namespacevt_a0f8ba6b65b1d630ab97265dcc90369a9}{Msg\+Ptr} (and all copies of such) are destroyed.

\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Create a new message, of a size. 

Create a new message already wrapped in a \doxylink{namespacevt_a0f8ba6b65b1d630ab97265dcc90369a9}{Msg\+Ptr}. The arguments are forwarded down to the underlying message\textquotesingle{}s constructor.

The lifetime of the message is controlled by \doxylink{namespacevt_a0f8ba6b65b1d630ab97265dcc90369a9}{Msg\+Ptr} and will be destroyed when the returned \doxylink{namespacevt_a0f8ba6b65b1d630ab97265dcc90369a9}{Msg\+Ptr} (and all copies of such) are destroyed.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacevt_a43e732a913e6062ff88012f15816526d} 
std::\+unique\+\_\+ptr$<$ \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structvt_1_1_startup_config}{Startup\+Config}} $>$ vt::\+preconfigure (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int \&}]{argc}{, }\item[{char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\&}]{argv}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Preconfigure VT with argc/\+argv. This will remove all VT arguments and create a {\ttfamily \doxylink{structvt_1_1_startup_config}{Startup\+Config}} for VT that should be passed to {\ttfamily initialize\+Preconfigured}. Optionally, one may specify an MPI communicator to use (otherwise, it defaults to {\ttfamily MPI\+\_\+\+COMM\+\_\+\+WORLD}). 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
MPI must be initialized to call this function because if an error occurs it uses MPI rank to limit how many times the error text gets printed.
\end{DoxyNote}

\end{DoxyParams}

Wrap a message as a \doxylink{namespacevt_a0f8ba6b65b1d630ab97265dcc90369a9}{Msg\+Ptr$<$\+Msg$>$}, increasing ref-\/ownership. 

This is the same as using \doxylink{namespacevt_a0f8ba6b65b1d630ab97265dcc90369a9}{Msg\+Ptr$<$\+T$>$}\{T\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\} directly. The primary usage is in historic call-\/sites as new code should prefer using {\ttfamily make\+Message} (and accepting a \doxylink{namespacevt_a0f8ba6b65b1d630ab97265dcc90369a9}{Msg\+Ptr}) instead of creating a raw message first. \Hypertarget{namespacevt_aa007f7ca7da0ec47b5d4ae7f03537fe3}\index{vt@{vt}!promoteMsgOwner@{promoteMsgOwner}}
